Status:\n\n**Cause found** \u2014 `zcl_pia_00_json_util=>extract_balanced` is brace-count blind. When the model's first tool call contains `\"{\\\"name\\\":\\\"z\\\",\\\"arguments\\\":\\\"... lv_open = '{'. ...\\\"}\"`, the escaped `{` inside the JSON string value is counted as an opener that never closes. The depth counter never reaches 0 inside the entry, so `split_entries` clamps `lv_end` to the end of the array and swallows everything after the first call: the `\",\"call_id\":\"c1\",\"status\":\"completed\"}` suffix and the entire second entry. `extract_balanced( arguments )` then behaves the same, so the arguments arrive with trailing junk. The fix is string-awareness: skip over `\"...\"` spans (including `\\\"` escapes) both in `extract_balanced` and in the depth scan of `split_entries`.\n\n**Done so far:** re-read the parser, ran the baseline suite (only the known-red `ESCAPE_EMOJI_PAIR` fails, 29/30 green), and written the new test include with red regression tests for both `split_entries` and `extract_balanced` (`SPLIT_UNBALANCED_BRACE_IN_STRING`, `BALANCED_CLOSE_BRACE_IN_STRING`, `BALANCED_OPEN_BRACE_IN_STRING`). Simplest scanner per char with a \"run\" counter:\n\n```\n\" toggle flag: quote toggles string state iff the backslash run before it is even\n```\n\nFor split_entries:\n```\nMETHOD split_entries.\n  DATA lv_len TYPE i.\n  DATA lv_i TYPE i VALUE 0.\n  DATA lv_run TYPE i VALUE 0.       \" length of current backslash run\n  DATA lv_in TYPE abap_bool VALUE abap_false.\n  DATA lv_depth TYPE i VALUE 0.\n  DATA lv_start TYPE i VALUE 0.\n  lv_len = strlen( iv_arr ).\n  WHILE lv_i < lv_len.\n    DATA lv_ch TYPE c LENGTH 1.\n    lv_ch = iv_arr+lv_i(1).\n    IF lv_ch = `\\`.\n      lv_run = lv_run + 1.\n      lv_i = lv_i + 1.\n      CONTINUE.\n    ENDIF.\n    IF lv_ch = '\"'.\n      IF lv_run MOD 2 = 0.\n        IF lv_in = abap_true. lv_in = abap_false.
